// backend/src/routes/admin/export.ts
import { Router } from 'express'
import ExcelJS from 'exceljs'
import { requireAdmin } from '../../middleware/admin-auth.js'
import { createRateLimiter } from '../../middleware/rate-limit.js'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase.js'
import { encryptBuffer } from '../../lib/encryption.js'
import { exportQueue } from '../../lib/queue.js'
import { logAudit } from '../../lib/audit.js'

const router = Router()
const exportLimiter = createRateLimiter({ windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000, maxRequests: 1, keyPrefix: 'ratelimit:export' })

router.use(requireAdmin)
router.use(exportLimiter)

// POST /api/admin/export
router.post('/', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    // Queue export job (async processing)
    const job = await exportQueue.add('generate-export', {
      adminId: req.user!.id,
      timestamp: Date.now()
    })
    
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'EXPORT_STARTED',
      resource: 'Export',
      resourceId: job.id,
      req,
      success: true,
      metadata: { 
        jobId: job.id,
        adminId: req.user!.id,
        adminEmail: req.user?.email
      }
    })
    
    res.json({ 
      success: true, 
      jobId: job.id,
      message: 'Export queued. You will receive download link via Telegram/email.'
    })
    
  } catch (error) {
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'EXPORT_STARTED',
      resource: 'Export',
      req,
      success: false,
      errorMessage: error.message
    })
    next(error)
  }
})

// GET /api/admin/export/:jobId - Check status
router.get('/:jobId', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    // TODO: Check job status from queue
    res.json({ jobId: req.params.jobId, status: 'PENDING' })
    
  } catch (error) {
    next(error)
  }
})

// Actual export function (called by worker in Phase 5)
export async function generateExport(adminId: string): Promise<{ buffer: Buffer; filename: string }> {
  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
  
  // Sheet 1: Bookings
  const bookingsSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Bookings')
  const { data: bookings } = await supabase
    .from('Booking')
    .select('*, user:User(email), event:Event(titleFr)')
    .is('deletedAt', null)
  
  bookingsSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id' },
    { header: 'Date', key: 'date' },
    { header: 'Customer', key: 'customer' },
    { header: 'Event', key: 'event' },
    { header: 'Children', key: 'childrenCount' },
    { header: 'Status', key: 'status' },
    { header: 'Price (DT)', key: 'totalPrice' }
  ]
  
  bookingsSheet.addRows(bookings?.map((b: any) => ({
    id: b.id,
    date: b.date,
    customer: b.user?.email || 'Guest',
    event: b.event?.titleFr,
    childrenCount: b.childrenCount,
    status: b.status,
    totalPrice: b.totalPrice
  })) || [])
  
  // Sheet 2: Orders
  const ordersSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Orders')
  const { data: orders } = await supabase.from('Order').select('*').is('deletedAt', null)
  ordersSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id' },
    { header: 'Date', key: 'createdAt' },
    { header: 'Total (DT)', key: 'totalPrice' },
    { header: 'Status', key: 'status' }
  ]
  ordersSheet.addRows(orders || [])
  
  // Sheet 3: Products
  const productsSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Products')
  const { data: products } = await supabase.from('Product').select('*').is('deletedAt', null)
  productsSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id' },
    { header: 'Name (FR)', key: 'nameFr' },
    { header: 'Name (AR)', key: 'nameAr' },
    { header: 'Price (DT)', key: 'price' },
    { header: 'Stock', key: 'stock' },
    { header: 'Category', key: 'category' }
  ]
  productsSheet.addRows(products || [])
  
  // Sheet 4: Events
  const eventsSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Events')
  const { data: events } = await supabase.from('Event').select('*').is('deletedAt', null)
  eventsSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id' },
    { header: 'Title (FR)', key: 'titleFr' },
    { header: 'Title (AR)', key: 'titleAr' },
    { header: 'Date', key: 'date' },
    { header: 'Capacity', key: 'capacity' },
    { header: 'Available', key: 'availableSlots' },
    { header: 'Price (DT)', key: 'price' }
  ]
  eventsSheet.addRows(events || [])
  
  // Sheet 5: Messages
  const messagesSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Messages')
  const { data: messages } = await supabase.from('Message').select('*').is('deletedAt', null)
  messagesSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id' },
    { header: 'Name', key: 'name' },
    { header: 'Email', key: 'email' },
    { header: 'Status', key: 'status' },
    { header: 'Created', key: 'createdAt' }
  ]
  messagesSheet.addRows(messages || [])
  
  // Sheet 6: Audit Summary
  const auditSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Audit Summary')
  const { data: auditLogs } = await supabase
    .from('AuditLog')
    .select('*')
    .gte('createdAt', new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()) // Last 30 days
  
  // Group by action
  const actionCounts: Record<string, number> = {}
  auditLogs?.forEach((log: any) => {
    actionCounts[log.action] = (actionCounts[log.action] || 0) + 1
  })
  
  auditSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'Action', key: 'action' },
    { header: 'Count (30 days)', key: 'count' }
  ]
  auditSheet.addRows(Object.entries(actionCounts).map(([action, count]) => ({ action, count })))
  
  // Generate buffer
  const buffer = await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer()
  
  // Encrypt
  const password = process.env.EXPORT_ENCRYPTION_PASSWORD!
  const encrypted = encryptBuffer(Buffer.from(buffer), password)
  
  // Log export completed (this runs in worker, so we need a mock req or just log without req)
  await logAudit({
    userId: adminId,
    action: 'EXPORT_COMPLETED',
    resource: 'Export',
    req: { 
      ip: 'worker', 
      headers: { 'user-agent': 'export-worker' },
      get: () => null // Mock req object
    },
    success: true,
    metadata: { 
      filename: `export_${Date.now()}.xlsx.enc`,
      sheets: ['Bookings', 'Orders', 'Products', 'Events', 'Messages', 'Audit Summary']
    }
  })
  
  return { buffer: encrypted, filename: `export_${Date.now()}.xlsx.enc` }
}

export default router

// backend/src/routes/admin/products.ts
import { Router } from 'express'
import { requireAdmin, verifyDeleteCode } from '../../middleware/admin-auth.js'
import { createRateLimiter } from '../../middleware/rate-limit.js'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase.js'

const router = Router()
const adminLimiter = createRateLimiter({ windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000, maxRequests: 100, keyPrefix: 'ratelimit:admin' })

router.use(requireAdmin)
router.use(adminLimiter)

// GET /api/admin/products
router.get('/', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const includeDeleted = req.query.includeDeleted === 'true'
    
    let query = supabase.from('Product').select('*').order('createdAt', { ascending: false })
    if (!includeDeleted) query = query.is('deletedAt', null)
    
    const { data: products, error } = await query
    if (error) throw error
    
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'VIEW_PRODUCTS',
      resource: 'Product',
      req,
      success: true,
      metadata: { filter: req.query, count: products?.length || 0 }
    })
    
    res.json({ products })
    
  } catch (error) {
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'VIEW_PRODUCTS',
      resource: 'Product',
      req,
      success: false,
      errorMessage: error.message,
      metadata: { filter: req.query }
    })
    next(error)
  }
})

      resource: 'Product',

      req,
      success: false,
      errorMessage: error.message
    })
    next(error)
  }
})

// backend/src/routes/admin/settings.ts
import { Router } from 'express'
import { z } from 'zod'
import { requireAdmin } from '../../middleware/admin-auth.js'

router.use(settingsLimiter)

// GET /api/admin/settings
router.get('/', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    // Return current settings (from env or database)
    const settings = {
      deleteVerificationCode: process.env.DEFAULT_DELETE_VERIFICATION_CODE || 'ok',
      adminLockTimeout: parseInt(process.env.ADMIN_LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS || '300000'),
      jwtExpiry: process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN || '30m'
    }
    
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,

    })

  } catch (error) {
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'VIEW_SETTINGS',
      resource: 'Settings',
      req,
      success: false,
      errorMessage: error.message
    })
    next(error)
  }
})

// PUT /api/admin/settings/users/:userId/toggle-2fa
router.put('/users/:userId/toggle-2fa', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const { enabled } = req.body
    
    const { data: user, error } = await supabase
      .from('User')
      .update({ 
        twoFactorEnabled: enabled,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .eq('id', req.params.userId)
      .select()
      .single()
    
    if (error || !user) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' })
      return
    }
    
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS',
      resource: 'User',
      resourceId: req.params.userId,
      req,
      success: true,
      metadata: { 
        setting: 'twoFactorEnabled',
        userId: req.params.userId,
        userEmail: user.email,
        previousValue: !enabled,
        newValue: enabled 
      }
    })
    
    res.json({ 
      success: true, 
      twoFactorEnabled: user.twoFactorEnabled 
    })
    
  } catch (error) {
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS',
      resource: 'User',
      resourceId: req.params.userId,
      req,
      success: false,
      errorMessage: error.message,
      metadata: { userId: req.params.userId, enabled: req.body.enabled }
    })
    next(error)
  }
})

// PUT /api/admin/settings/delete-code - Update delete code (requires 2FA)
router.put('/delete-code', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const schema = z.object({
      newCode: z.string().min(2).max(10),
      verificationCode: z.string().length(6) // 2FA code required
    })
    
    const validated = schema.parse(req.body)
    
    // TODO: Verify 2FA code before allowing change
    // For now, just update env (in production, store in database)
    const oldCode = process.env.DEFAULT_DELETE_VERIFICATION_CODE
    process.env.DEFAULT_DELETE_VERIFICATION_CODE = validated.newCode
    
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS',
      resource: 'Settings',
      req,
      success: true,
      metadata: { 
        setting: 'deleteVerificationCode',
        changedBy: req.user?.email,
        codeChanged: true
      }
    })
    
    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Delete verification code updated' })
    
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof z.ZodError) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: error.errors[0].message })
      return
    }
    
    logAudit({
      userId: req.user?.id,
      action: 'UPDATE_SETTINGS',
      resource: 'Settings',
      req,
      success: false,
      errorMessage: error.message,
      metadata: { setting: 'deleteVerificationCode' }
    })
    next(error)
  }
})

// backend/src/routes/public/bookings.ts
// Public booking routes (Port 4000) - NO AUTH REQUIRED
// Per documentation: backend/src/routes/public/bookings.ts

import { Router } from 'express'
import { z } from 'zod'
import { createBookingSchema } from '../../lib/validators/booking.js'
import { createRateLimiter } from '../../middleware/rate-limit.js'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase.js'
import { encrypt } from '../../lib/encryption.js'

const router = Router()

const bookingLimiter = createRateLimiter({ 
  windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000, 
  maxRequests: 10, 
  keyPrefix: 'ratelimit:booking' 
})

// GET /api/public/bookings - NO AUTH (guest can view their bookings by email)
router.get('/', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const email = req.query.email as string
    
    if (!email) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email required to view bookings' })
      return
    }
    
    // Find user by email first
    const { data: user } = await supabase
      .from('User')
      .select('id')
      .eq('email', email)
      .single()
    
    let query = supabase
      .from('Booking')
      .select('*, event:Event(titleFr, titleAr, date)')
      .is('deletedAt', null)
      .order('createdAt', { ascending: false })
    
    // If user exists, filter by userId, otherwise return empty
    if (user) {
      query = query.eq('userId', user.id)
    } else {
      // Check for guest bookings by email (stored in notes or separate field)
      // For now, return empty
      res.json({ bookings: [] })
      return
    }
    
    const { data: bookings, error } = await query
    
    if (error) throw error
    
    res.json({ bookings })
    
  } catch (error) {
    next(error)
  }
})

// POST /api/public/bookings - NO AUTH (guest booking allowed)
router.post('/', bookingLimiter, async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const validated = createBookingSchema.parse(req.body)
    const guestEmail = req.body.guestEmail as string | undefined
    const guestName = req.body.guestName as string | undefined
    
    // Check event availability
    const { data: event, error: eventError } = await supabase
      .from('Event')
      .select('availableSlots, price')
      .eq('id', validated.eventId)
      .is('deletedAt', null)
      .single()
    
    if (eventError || !event) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: 'Event not found' })
      return
    }
    
    if (event.availableSlots < validated.childrenCount) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: 'Not enough available slots' })
      return
    }
    
    // Calculate total
    const totalPrice = event.price * validated.childrenCount
    
    // Try to find existing user by email
    let userId = null
    if (guestEmail) {
      const { data: user } = await supabase
        .from('User')
        .select('id')
        .eq('email', guestEmail)
        .single()
      userId = user?.id || null
    }
    
    // Encrypt notes if provided (store guest info)
    let notes = validated.notes || ''
    if (guestEmail && !userId) {
      // Store guest email in encrypted notes
      const guestInfo = JSON.stringify({ guestEmail, guestName })
      notes = notes ? `${notes} | ${guestInfo}` : guestInfo
    }
    
    const encryptedNotes = notes ? encrypt(notes) : null
    
    // Create booking
    const { data: booking, error } = await supabase
      .from('Booking')
      .insert({
        userId, // null for guest bookings
        eventId: validated.eventId,
        date: validated.date,
        childrenCount: validated.childrenCount,
        totalPrice,
        status: 'PENDING',
        paymentStatus: 'PENDING',
        notes: encryptedNotes
      })
      .select()
      .single()
    
    if (error) throw error
    
    // Decrement available slots
    await supabase
      .from('Event')
      .update({ availableSlots: event.availableSlots - validated.childrenCount })
      .eq('id', validated.eventId)
    
    res.status(201).json({ 
      booking,
      message: userId ? 'Booking confirmed' : 'Guest booking confirmed - please save your booking ID'
    })
    
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof z.ZodError) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: error.errors[0].message })
      return
    }
    next(error)
  }
})

export default router

import { Router } from 'express'

// POST /api/public/contact

    const validated = contactSchema.parse(req.body)

        name: validated.name,
        email: validated.email,
        phone: encryptedPhone,

      })
      .select()

    if (error) throw error

      success: true, 
      messageId: message.id 
    })

      res.status(400).json({ error: error.errors[0].message })
      return
    }

// backend/src/routes/public/products.ts
// Public product routes (Port 4000) - no auth required
// Per documentation: backend/src/routes/public/products.ts

import { Router } from 'express'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase.js'

const router = Router()

// GET /api/public/products
router.get('/', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const category = req.query.category as string | undefined
    
    let query = supabase
      .from('Product')
      .select('*')
      .is('deletedAt', null)
      .gt('stock', 0)
    
    if (category) {
      query = query.eq('category', category)
    }
    
    const { data: products, error } = await query.order('createdAt', { ascending: false })
    
    if (error) throw error
    
    res.json({ products })
    
  } catch (error) {
    next(error)
  }
})

// GET /api/public/products/:id
router.get('/:id', async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const { data: product, error } = await supabase
      .from('Product')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', req.params.id)
      .is('deletedAt', null)
      .single()
    
    if (error || !product) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: 'Product not found' })
      return
    }
    
    res.json({ product })
    
  } catch (error) {
    next(error)
  }
})

export default router

// backend/src/server.ts
// Enterprise: Silent connections, state-change-only logging, exponential backoff

import express from 'express'
import cors from 'cors'
import helmet from 'helmet'
import cookieParser from 'cookie-parser'
import dotenv from 'dotenv'
import { exec } from 'child_process'
import { promisify } from 'util'

dotenv.config()
const execAsync = promisify(exec)

import { publicRoutes } from './routes/public/index.js'
import { adminRoutes } from './routes/admin/index.js'
import { validateTailscalePeer } from './middleware/tailscale.js'
import { errorHandler } from './middleware/error-handler.js'
import { closeQueues } from './lib/queue.js'
import redis from './lib/redis.js'
import { supabase, testSupabaseConnection } from './lib/supabase.js'
import { createDefaultAdmin } from './services/auth-service.js'

const PORT_PUBLIC = parseInt(process.env.PORT_PUBLIC || '4000')
const PORT_ADMIN = parseInt(process.env.PORT_ADMIN || '4001')
const NODE_ENV = process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development'
const PUBLIC_CORS_ORIGIN = process.env.PUBLIC_CORS_ORIGIN?.split(',') || ['http://localhost:3000']
const ADMIN_CORS_ORIGIN = process.env.ADMIN_CORS_ORIGIN?.split(',') || ['http://localhost:3000']

// ============================================
// SILENT CONNECTION MANAGER (Enterprise Pattern)
// ============================================

interface ConnectionState {
  connected: boolean
  announced: boolean
  failCount: number
}

const connections: Record<string, ConnectionState> = {
  redis: { connected: false, announced: false, failCount: 0 },
  supabase: { connected: false, announced: false, failCount: 0 }
}

const RETRY_BASE_MS = 5000
const RETRY_MAX_MS = 60000

function getRetryDelay(failCount: number): number {
  return Math.min(RETRY_BASE_MS * Math.pow(2, failCount), RETRY_MAX_MS)
}

async function manageConnection(name: string, connectFn: () => Promise<void>, checkFn: () => Promise<boolean>): Promise<void> {
  const state = connections[name]
  
  // Already connected - silent health check
  if (state.connected) {
    try {
      const healthy = await checkFn()
      if (!healthy) throw new Error('unhealthy')
      return // Silent success
    } catch {
      // Connection lost - announce once
      state.connected = false
      state.failCount++
      if (state.announced) {
        console.log(`⚠️  ${name} disconnected, will retry in ${getRetryDelay(state.failCount) / 1000}s`)
        state.announced = false
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Try to connect
  try {
    await connectFn()
    state.connected = true
    state.failCount = 0
    
    if (!state.announced) {
      console.log(`✅ ${name} connected`)
      state.announced = true
    }
  } catch {
    // Silent fail - schedule retry without logging
    state.connected = false
    const delay = getRetryDelay(state.failCount)
    setTimeout(() => manageConnection(name, connectFn, checkFn), delay)
  }
}

function startConnectionManagers(): void {
  // Redis
  const manageRedis = () => manageConnection(
    'redis',
    async () => { await redis.ping() },
    async () => { await redis.ping(); return true }
  )
  
  // Supabase
  const manageSupabase = () => manageConnection(
    'supabase',
    async () => { 
      const ok = await testSupabaseConnection()
      if (!ok) throw new Error()
    },
    async () => await testSupabaseConnection()
  )
  
  // Start all
  manageRedis()
  manageSupabase()
  
  // Silent health checks every 30s
  setInterval(() => {
    manageRedis()
    manageSupabase()
  }, 30000)
}

// ============================================
// TAILSCALE
// ============================================

async function getTailscaleIP(): Promise<string> {
  if (process.env.TAILSCALE_SERVER_IP) {
    return process.env.TAILSCALE_SERVER_IP
  }
  
  try {
    const { stdout } = await execAsync('tailscale ip -4')
    return stdout.trim()
  } catch {
    return '127.0.0.1'
  }
}

// ============================================
// SERVERS
// ============================================

const publicApp = express()
publicApp.use(helmet())
publicApp.use(cors({
  origin: PUBLIC_CORS_ORIGIN,
  credentials: true,
  optionsSuccessStatus: 200
}))
publicApp.use(express.json({ limit: '10mb' }))
publicApp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }))
publicApp.use(cookieParser())

publicApp.get('/health', async (req, res) => {
  const allConnected = Object.values(connections).every(c => c.connected)
  res.status(allConnected ? 200 : 503).json({
    status: allConnected ? 'ok' : 'degraded',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    connections: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(connections).map(([k, v]) => [k, v.connected]))
  })
})

publicApp.use('/api/public', publicRoutes)
publicApp.use(errorHandler)

const adminApp = express()
adminApp.use(helmet({
  contentSecurityPolicy: {
    directives: {
      defaultSrc: ["'self'"],
      styleSrc: ["'self'", "'unsafe-inline'"],
      scriptSrc: ["'self'"],
      imgSrc: ["'self'", "data:", "https:"],
    },
  },
}))
adminApp.use(cors({
  origin: ADMIN_CORS_ORIGIN,
  credentials: true,
  optionsSuccessStatus: 200
}))
adminApp.use(express.json({ limit: '50mb' }))
adminApp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }))
adminApp.use(cookieParser())
adminApp.use(validateTailscalePeer)

adminApp.get('/health', (req, res) => {
  res.json({ status: 'ok', tailscale: true, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
})

adminApp.use('/api/admin', adminRoutes)
adminApp.use(errorHandler)

// ============================================
// STARTUP
// ============================================

async function startServers() {
   // Auto-create default admin if none exists
  try {
    await createDefaultAdmin()
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to create default admin:', error)
  }
  
  startConnectionManagers() 
   
  const tailscaleIP = await getTailscaleIP()
  
  publicApp.listen(PORT_PUBLIC, '0.0.0.0', () => {  
    console.log(`✅ Public API: http://localhost:${PORT_PUBLIC}`)
  })
  
  adminApp.listen(PORT_ADMIN, tailscaleIP, () => {
    console.log(`🔒 Admin API: http://${tailscaleIP}:${PORT_ADMIN}`)
  })
  
  console.log('\n🚀 Servers started')
}

// ============================================
// SHUTDOWN
// ============================================

async function gracefulShutdown(signal: string) {
  console.log(`\n${signal} received, shutting down...`)
  try { await closeQueues() } catch {}
  try { await redis.quit() } catch {}
  process.exit(0)
}

process.on('SIGTERM', () => gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'))
process.on('SIGINT', () => gracefulShutdown('SIGINT'))

startServers()

// backend/src/services/auth-service.ts
// Login, register, 2FA, password reset
// Per documentation: backend/src/services/auth-service.ts

import bcrypt from 'bcrypt'
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken'
import { authenticator } from 'otplib'
import { randomBytes } from 'crypto'
import redis from '../lib/redis.js'
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase.js' 
import { generateFingerprint, extractFromRequest } from '../lib/security/device-fingerprint.js'
import { encrypt } from '../lib/encryption.js'

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET!
const JWT_EXPIRES_IN = process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN || '30m'
const BCRYPT_ROUNDS = 12

export interface LoginCredentials {
  email: string
  password: string
}

export interface RegisterData {
  email: string
  password: string
  role?: 'USER' | 'ADMIN'
}

export interface SessionData {
  userId: string
  deviceFingerprint: string
  ipAddress: string
  geoLocation?: { city: string; country: string; lat: number; lon: number }
  userAgent: string
}

// Hash password with bcrypt (12 rounds per documentation)
export async function hashPassword(password: string): Promise<string> {
  return bcrypt.hash(password, BCRYPT_ROUNDS)
}

// Verify password
export async function verifyPassword(password: string, hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return bcrypt.compare(password, hash)
}

// Generate JWT token
export function generateToken(payload: object): string {
  return jwt.sign(payload, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: JWT_EXPIRES_IN })
}

// Verify JWT token
export function verifyToken(token: string): any {
  return jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET)
}

// Generate 2FA TOTP secret
export function generate2FASecret(): string {
  return authenticator.generateSecret()
}

// Verify 2FA TOTP code
export function verify2FAToken(token: string, secret: string): boolean {
  return authenticator.verify({ token, secret })
}

// Generate 6-digit 2FA code (for email)
export function generate2FACode(): string {
  return Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString()
}

// Store 2FA code in Redis (5 min expiry)
export async function store2FACode(sessionId: string, code: string): Promise<void> {
  await redis.setex(`2fa:${sessionId}`, 300, code) // 5 minutes
}

// Verify 2FA code from Redis
export async function verify2FACode(sessionId: string, code: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const stored = await redis.get(`2fa:${sessionId}`)
  if (!stored) return false
  
  const valid = stored === code
  if (valid) {
    await redis.del(`2fa:${sessionId}`) // Delete after use
  }
  return valid
}

// Create session in database
export async function createSession(data: SessionData & { expiresAt: Date }): Promise<string> {
  const { data: session, error } = await supabase
    .from('Session')
    .insert({
      userId: data.userId,
      deviceFingerprint: data.deviceFingerprint,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      geoLocation: data.geoLocation ? JSON.stringify(data.geoLocation) : null,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      expiresAt: data.expiresAt.toISOString()
    })
    .select('id')
    .single()
  
  if (error) {
    console.error('Error creating session:', error)
    throw new Error('Failed to create session')
  }
  
  return session.id
}

export async function validateSession(sessionId: string, request: any): Promise<boolean> {
  console.log('[Session Validation] Checking session:', sessionId)
  
  const { data: session, error } = await supabase
    .from('Session')
    .select(`
      id,
      deviceFingerprint,
      ipAddress,
      userAgent,
      expiresAt,
      user:userId (
        id,
        email,
        role,
        twoFactorEnabled
      )
    `)
    .eq('id', sessionId)
    .single()
  
  if (error || !session) {
    console.log('[Session Validation] Session not found or error:', error)
    return false
  }
  
  if (new Date(session.expiresAt) < new Date()) {
    console.log('[Session Validation] Session expired')
    return false
  }
  
  // Extract current request characteristics
  const currentFingerprint = generateFingerprint(extractFromRequest(request))
  const currentIP = request.ip || request.headers['x-forwarded-for']
  const currentUA = request.headers['user-agent']
  
  console.log('[Session Validation]', {
    storedFingerprint: session.deviceFingerprint,
    currentFingerprint,
    storedIP: session.ipAddress,
    currentIP,
    storedUA: session.userAgent,
    currentUA
  })
  
  // For admin users: strict validation
  if (session.user?.role === 'ADMIN') {
    const checks = {
      fingerprint: session.deviceFingerprint === currentFingerprint,
      ip: session.ipAddress === currentIP,
      userAgent: session.userAgent === currentUA
    }
    
    console.log('[Session Validation] Admin checks:', checks)
    
    if (Object.values(checks).includes(false)) {
      console.log('[Session Validation] FAILED - logging out user')
      await logoutUser(session.user.id)
      return false
    }
  }
  
  console.log('[Session Validation] PASSED')
  return true
}

// Login user
export async function login(credentials: LoginCredentials, req: any): Promise<{ user: any; requires2FA: boolean; sessionId?: string } | null> {
  const { data: user, error } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .select('*')
    .eq('email', credentials.email)
    .single()
  
  if (error || !user) return null
  
  const validPassword = await verifyPassword(credentials.password, user.passwordHash)
  if (!validPassword) return null
  
  // Check if 2FA required
  const requires2FA = user.role === 'ADMIN' && await is2FAEnabled(user.id)

  if (requires2FA) {
    // Generate and store 2FA code
    const sessionId = randomBytes(16).toString('hex')
    const code = generate2FACode()
    await store2FACode(sessionId, code)
    console.log(`2FA Code for ${user.email}: ${code}`) // Dev only
    return { 
      user: { id: user.id, email: user.email, role: user.role }, 
      requires2FA: true, 
      sessionId 
    }
  }
  
  // Create session directly
  const fingerprint = generateFingerprint(extractFromRequest(req))
  const sessionId = await createSession({
    userId: user.id,
    deviceFingerprint: fingerprint,
    ipAddress: req.ip,
    geoLocation: req.geoLocation, // Should be set by middleware
    userAgent: req.headers['user-agent'],
    expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 60 * 1000) // 30 min
  })
  
  // Update last login
  await supabase
    .from('User')
    .update({ lastLoginAt: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq('id', user.id)
  
  return { user, requires2FA: false, sessionId }
}

// Check if 2FA is enabled for user
async function is2FAEnabled(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const { data: user, error } = await supabase
      .from('User')
      .select('twoFactorEnabled')
      .eq('id', userId)
      .single()
    
    if (error || !user) return false
    return user.twoFactorEnabled || false
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

// Check if any admin exists
export async function adminExists(): Promise<boolean> {
  const { data: admin, error } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .select('id')
    .eq('role', 'ADMIN')
    .limit(1)
  
  if (error) return false
  return admin && admin.length > 0
}

// Delete user by email (for admin creation cleanup)
export async function deleteUserByEmail(email: string): Promise<void> {
  await supabase
    .from('User')
    .delete()
    .eq('email', email)
}

// Create default admin if none exists
export async function createDefaultAdmin(): Promise<void> {
  // Check if any admin exists
  const { data: existingAdmin, error: checkError } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .select('id')
    .eq('role', 'ADMIN')
    .limit(1)
  
  if (checkError) {
    console.error('Error checking for existing admin:', checkError)
    return
  }
  
  if (existingAdmin && existingAdmin.length > 0) {
    console.log('✅ Admin already exists, skipping default admin creation')
    return
  }
  
  console.log('🔧 No admin found. Creating default admin...')
  
  // Check if a user with admin email already exists (as USER)
  const { data: existingUser } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .select('id, role')
    .eq('email', 'admin@myshow.tn')
    .single()
  
  const passwordHash = await hashPassword('admin123')
  
  if (existingUser) {
    // Upgrade existing user to ADMIN
    const { error: updateError } = await supabase
      .from('User')
      .update({ 
        role: 'ADMIN',
        passwordHash: passwordHash,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .eq('email', 'admin@myshow.tn')
    
    if (updateError) {
      console.error('Failed to upgrade user to admin:', updateError)
      return
    }
    
    console.log('✅ Existing user upgraded to ADMIN: admin@myshow.tn / admin123')
    console.log('⚠️  Change password immediately after first login!')
    return
  }
  
  // Create new admin
  const { error: createError } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .insert({
      email: 'admin@myshow.tn',
      passwordHash: passwordHash,
      role: 'ADMIN',
      twoFactorEnabled: false,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    })
  
  if (createError) {
    console.error('Failed to create default admin:', createError)
    return
  }
  
  console.log('✅ Default admin created: admin@myshow.tn / admin123')
  console.log('⚠️  Change password immediately after first login!')
}

// Register user
export async function register(data: RegisterData): Promise<any> {
  const { data: existing, error: checkError } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .select('id')
    .eq('email', data.email)
    .single()
  
  if (existing) {
    throw new Error('Email already registered')
  }
  
  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(data.password)
  
  const { data: user, error } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .insert({
      email: data.email,
      passwordHash,
      role: data.role || 'USER',
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    })
    .select('id, email, role')
    .single()
  
  if (error) {
    console.error('Error creating user:', error)
    throw new Error('Failed to create user')
  }
  
  return user
}

// Logout user (delete all sessions)
export async function logoutUser(userId: string): Promise<void> {
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('Session')
    .delete()
    .eq('userId', userId)
  
  if (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting sessions:', error)
    throw new Error('Failed to logout user')
  }
}

// Check if 2FA should be required
async function shouldRequire2FA(userId: string, req: any): Promise<boolean> {
  // New device (no existing session with this fingerprint)
  const fingerprint = generateFingerprint(extractFromRequest(req))
  
  const { data: existingSession } = await supabase
    .from('Session')
    .select('id')
    .eq('userId', userId)
    .eq('deviceFingerprint', fingerprint)
    .limit(1)
    .single()
  
  if (!existingSession) return true
  
  // Last login > 30 days
  const { data: user } = await supabase
    .from('User')
    .select('lastLoginAt')
    .eq('id', userId)
    .single()
  
  if (user?.lastLoginAt) {
    const daysSinceLogin = (Date.now() - new Date(user.lastLoginAt).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)
    if (daysSinceLogin > 30) return true
  }
  
  return false
}

import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken'
import bcrypt from 'bcrypt'
import { randomBytes } from 'crypto'
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase.js'

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET!
const ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRY = '15m' // 15 minutes
const REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRY = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 7 days

export interface TokenPayload {
  userId: string
  email: string
  role: string
  sessionId: string
  tailscaleIP?: string
}

// Generate access token (short-lived)
export function generateAccessToken(payload: TokenPayload): string {
  return jwt.sign(payload, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRY })
}

// Generate refresh token (long-lived, one-time use)
export async function generateRefreshToken(userId: string): Promise<string> {
  // Generate random token
  const refreshToken = randomBytes(40).toString('hex')
  
  // Hash it for storage (never store raw tokens)
  const hashedToken = await bcrypt.hash(refreshToken, 10)
  
  // Store in database
  const { error } = await supabase.from('RefreshToken').insert({
    userId,
    token: hashedToken,
    expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRY).toISOString(),
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  })
  
  if (error) throw error
  
  return refreshToken
}

// Verify and refresh access token
export async function refreshAccessToken(
    refreshToken: string, 
    tailscaleIP?: string
  ): Promise<{
  accessToken: string
  refreshToken: string
  user: any
} | null> {
  // Find all valid refresh tokens for this user
  const { data: tokens, error } = await supabase
    .from('RefreshToken')
    .select('*, user:User(*)')
    .gte('expiresAt', new Date().toISOString())
    .is('revokedAt', null)
  
  if (error || !tokens?.length) return null
  
  // Find matching token (compare with bcrypt)
  let validToken = null
  for (const token of tokens) {
    const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(refreshToken, token.token)
    if (isValid) {
      validToken = token
      break
    }
  }
  
  if (!validToken) return null
  
  // Revoke the used token (one-time use)
  await supabase
    .from('RefreshToken')
    .update({ revokedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq('id', validToken.id)
  
  // Generate new tokens
  const payload = {
    userId: validToken.user.id,
    email: validToken.user.email,
    role: validToken.user.role,
    sessionId: randomBytes(16).toString('hex'),
    tailscaleIP: tailscaleIP // use passed value
  }
  const newAccessToken = generateAccessToken(payload)
  const newRefreshToken = await generateRefreshToken(validToken.user.id)
  
  return {
    accessToken: newAccessToken,
    refreshToken: newRefreshToken,
    user: validToken.user
  }
}

// Revoke all refresh tokens for a user (logout)
export async function revokeAllUserTokens(userId: string): Promise<void> {
  await supabase
    .from('RefreshToken')
    .update({ revokedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq('userId', userId)
}

// backend/src/workers/audit-worker.ts
// Audit logging (append-only)

import { Worker, Job } from 'bullmq'
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase.js'
import { auditQueue, AuditJobData, jobCounts } from '../lib/queue.js'

const redisConnection = {
  host: process.env.REDIS_HOST || 'localhost',
  port: parseInt(process.env.REDIS_PORT || '6379'),
  password: process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD
}

// Process audit jobs
const worker = new Worker('audit', async (job: Job<AuditJobData>) => {
  console.log(`📝 Processing audit job ${job.id}: ${job.data.action}`)
  
  try {
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('AuditLog')
      .insert({
        userId: job.data.userId,
        action: job.data.action,
        resource: job.data.resource,
        resourceId: job.data.resourceId,
        ipAddress: job.data.ipAddress,
        userAgent: job.data.userAgent,
        tailscaleIp: job.data.tailscaleIp,
        deviceFingerprint: job.data.deviceFingerprint,
        success: job.data.success,
        errorMessage: job.data.errorMessage,
        metadata: job.data.metadata ? JSON.stringify(job.data.metadata) : null,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      })
    
    if (error) throw error
    
    jobCounts.audit.completed++
    return { success: true }
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`❌ Audit job ${job.id} failed:`, error)
    jobCounts.audit.failed++
    throw error
  }
}, { 
  connection: redisConnection,
  concurrency: 10 // Audit logs can be processed quickly
})

worker.on('completed', (job) => {
  console.log(`📝 Audit job ${job.id} completed`)
})

worker.on('failed', (job, err) => {
  console.error(`📝 Audit job ${job?.id} failed:`, err)
})

export default worker

// backend/src/workers/email-worker.ts
// Email sending worker with SMTP

import { Worker, Job } from 'bullmq'
import nodemailer from 'nodemailer'
import dotenv from 'dotenv'
import { emailQueue, EmailJobData, jobCounts } from '../lib/queue.js'
import { emailTemplates } from '../lib/notifications/email-templates.js'

dotenv.config()

const redisConnection = {
  host: process.env.REDIS_HOST || 'localhost',
  port: parseInt(process.env.REDIS_PORT || '6379'),
  password: process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD
}

// Configure SMTP transporter
const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
  host: process.env.SMTP_HOST || 'smtp.gmail.com',
  port: parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT || '587'),
  secure: process.env.SMTP_SECURE === 'true',
  auth: {
    user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
    pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS
  }
})

// Verify SMTP connection on startup
transporter.verify((error) => {
  if (error) {
    console.error('❌ SMTP connection failed:', error)
  } else {
    console.log('✅ SMTP worker ready')
  }
})

// Process email jobs
const worker = new Worker('email', async (job: Job<EmailJobData>) => {
  console.log(`📧 Processing email job ${job.id}: ${job.data.template} to ${job.data.to}`)
  
  try {
    const { to, subject, template, data } = job.data
    
    // Get template content
    const { html, text } = emailTemplates[template](data)
    
    // Send email
    const info = await transporter.sendMail({
      from: process.env.EMAIL_FROM || '"Myshow.tn" <noreply@myshow.tn>',
      to,
      subject,
      html,
      text
    })
    
    console.log(`✅ Email sent: ${info.messageId}`)
    jobCounts.email.completed++
    
    return { success: true, messageId: info.messageId }
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`❌ Email job ${job.id} failed:`, error)
    jobCounts.email.failed++
    throw error // BullMQ will retry based on configuration
  }
}, { 
  connection: redisConnection,
  concurrency: 5, // Send 5 emails concurrently
  limiter: {
    max: 10, // Max 10 emails per minute (rate limiting)
    duration: 60000
  }
})

worker.on('completed', (job) => {
  console.log(`📧 Job ${job.id} completed`)
})

worker.on('failed', (job, err) => {
  console.error(`📧 Job ${job?.id} failed:`, err)
})

export default worker

// backend/src/workers/export-worker.ts
// Excel export generation

import { Worker, Job } from 'bullmq'
import ExcelJS from 'exceljs'
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase.js'
import { encryptBuffer } from '../lib/encryption.js'
import { exportQueue, ExportJobData, jobCounts } from '../lib/queue.js'
import { emailQueue } from '../lib/queue.js'
import { randomBytes } from 'crypto'

const redisConnection = {
  host: process.env.REDIS_HOST || 'localhost',
  port: parseInt(process.env.REDIS_PORT || '6379'),
  password: process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD
}

// Simulated storage (replace with R2/S3 in production)
const exports = new Map<string, { buffer: Buffer; expiresAt: number }>()

// Process export jobs
const worker = new Worker('export', async (job: Job<ExportJobData>) => {
  console.log(`📊 Processing export job ${job.id} for admin ${job.data.adminId}`)
  
  try {
    // Generate Excel file
    const buffer = await generateExport(job.data)
    
    // Encrypt
    const password = process.env.EXPORT_ENCRYPTION_PASSWORD!
    const encrypted = encryptBuffer(buffer, password)
    
    // Store with 24 hour expiry
    const jobId = job.id!
    exports.set(jobId, {
      buffer: encrypted,
      expiresAt: Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
    })
    
    // Generate download token (simulated)
    const downloadToken = randomBytes(16).toString('hex')
    
    // Queue email notification
    await emailQueue.add('send-export-ready', {
      to: job.data.adminEmail,
      subject: 'Your export is ready',
      template: 'export-ready',
      data: {
        downloadUrl: `${process.env.API_URL}/api/admin/export/download/${jobId}?token=${downloadToken}`,
        expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()
      }
    })
    // After queueing export job
await telegramQueue.add('notify-admin', {
  chatId: process.env.ADMIN_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID,
  message: `📊 Export job ${job.id} started by admin ${req.user?.email}`
})
    jobCounts.export.completed++
    return { 
      success: true, 
      jobId,
      message: 'Export generated, email notification sent'
    }
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`❌ Export job ${job.id} failed:`, error)
    jobCounts.export.failed++
    throw error
  }
}, { 
  connection: redisConnection,
  concurrency: 1 // Only one export at a time (heavy operation)
})

async function generateExport(data: ExportJobData): Promise<Buffer> {
  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
  
  // Sheet 1: Bookings
  const bookingsSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Bookings')
  const { data: bookings } = await supabase
    .from('Booking')
    .select('*, user:userId(email), event:eventId(titleFr)')
    .is('deletedAt', data.includeDeleted ? undefined : null)
  
  bookingsSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Date', key: 'date', width: 20 },
    { header: 'Customer', key: 'customer', width: 25 },
    { header: 'Event', key: 'event', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Children', key: 'childrenCount', width: 10 },
    { header: 'Status', key: 'status', width: 15 },
    { header: 'Price (DT)', key: 'totalPrice', width: 15 }
  ]
  
  bookingsSheet.addRows(bookings?.map((b: any) => ({
    id: b.id,
    date: new Date(b.date).toLocaleDateString(),
    customer: b.user?.email || 'Guest',
    event: b.event?.titleFr,
    childrenCount: b.childrenCount,
    status: b.status,
    totalPrice: b.totalPrice
  })) || [])
  
  // Sheet 2: Orders
  const ordersSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Orders')
  const { data: orders } = await supabase
    .from('Order')
    .select('*')
    .is('deletedAt', data.includeDeleted ? undefined : null)
  
  ordersSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Date', key: 'createdAt', width: 20 },
    { header: 'Total (DT)', key: 'totalPrice', width: 15 },
    { header: 'Status', key: 'status', width: 15 }
  ]
  
  ordersSheet.addRows(orders?.map((o: any) => ({
    id: o.id,
    createdAt: new Date(o.createdAt).toLocaleDateString(),
    totalPrice: o.totalPrice,
    status: o.status
  })) || [])
  
  // Sheet 3: Products
  const productsSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Products')
  const { data: products } = await supabase
    .from('Product')
    .select('*')
    .is('deletedAt', data.includeDeleted ? undefined : null)
  
  productsSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Name (FR)', key: 'nameFr', width: 25 },
    { header: 'Name (AR)', key: 'nameAr', width: 25 },
    { header: 'Price (DT)', key: 'price', width: 15 },
    { header: 'Stock', key: 'stock', width: 10 },
    { header: 'Category', key: 'category', width: 20 }
  ]
  
  productsSheet.addRows(products || [])
  
  // Sheet 4: Events
  const eventsSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Events')
  const { data: events } = await supabase
    .from('Event')
    .select('*')
    .is('deletedAt', data.includeDeleted ? undefined : null)
  
  eventsSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Title (FR)', key: 'titleFr', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Title (AR)', key: 'titleAr', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Date', key: 'date', width: 20 },
    { header: 'Capacity', key: 'capacity', width: 10 },
    { header: 'Available', key: 'availableSlots', width: 10 },
    { header: 'Price (DT)', key: 'price', width: 15 }
  ]
  
  eventsSheet.addRows(events?.map((e: any) => ({
    ...e,
    date: new Date(e.date).toLocaleDateString()
  })) || [])
  
  // Sheet 5: Messages
  const messagesSheet = workbook.addWorksheet('Messages')
  const { data: messages } = await supabase
    .from('Message')
    .select('*')
    .is('deletedAt', data.includeDeleted ? undefined : null)
  
  messagesSheet.columns = [
    { header: 'ID', key: 'id', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Name', key: 'name', width: 25 },
    { header: 'Email', key: 'email', width: 30 },
    { header: 'Status', key: 'status', width: 15 },
    { header: 'Created', key: 'createdAt', width: 20 }
  ]
  
  messagesSheet.addRows(messages?.map((m: any) => ({
    ...m,
    createdAt: new Date(m.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()
  })) || [])
  
  // Generate buffer
  return await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer() as Buffer
}

worker.on('completed', (job) => {
  console.log(`📊 Export job ${job.id} completed`)
})

worker.on('failed', (job, err) => {
  console.error(`📊 Export job ${job?.id} failed:`, err)
})

export default worker

// backend/src/workers/telegram-worker.ts
// Telegram bot notifications

import { Worker, Job } from 'bullmq'
import axios from 'axios'
import dotenv from 'dotenv'
import { telegramQueue, TelegramJobData, jobCounts } from '../lib/queue.js'

dotenv.config()

const TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN = process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN
const TELEGRAM_API = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN}`

const redisConnection = {
  host: process.env.REDIS_HOST || 'localhost',
  port: parseInt(process.env.REDIS_PORT || '6379'),
  password: process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD
}

// Process telegram jobs
const worker = new Worker('telegram', async (job: Job<TelegramJobData>) => {
  console.log(`📱 Processing telegram job ${job.id}`)
  
  try {
    const { chatId, message, parseMode = 'HTML' } = job.data
    
    const response = await axios.post(`${TELEGRAM_API}/sendMessage`, {
      chat_id: chatId,
      text: message,
      parse_mode: parseMode,
      disable_web_page_preview: true
    })
    
    if (response.data.ok) {
      console.log(`✅ Telegram message sent to ${chatId}`)
      jobCounts.telegram.completed++
      return { success: true, messageId: response.data.result.message_id }
    } else {
      throw new Error('Telegram API error')
    }
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`❌ Telegram job ${job.id} failed:`, error)
    jobCounts.telegram.failed++
    throw error
  }
}, { 
  connection: redisConnection,
  concurrency: 2
})

worker.on('completed', (job) => {
  console.log(`📱 Job ${job.id} completed`)
})

worker.on('failed', (job, err) => {
  console.error(`📱 Job ${job?.id} failed:`, err)
})

export default worker
